ARBA00022806

View original ARBA rule on UniProt

Type: ARBA
Status: COMPLETE
Action: ACCEPT
Confidence: 0.75
Rule as retrieved: 2026-11-09
Condition sets examined: 335 of 335

Description

ARBA rule assigning the UniProt keyword Helicase (KW-0347) through 335 alternative condition sets built from InterPro, PANTHER and taxon conditions.

The domain overlap table is not shown for this review. It is drawn from a cached analysis of the rule's condition sets, and the cache does not match the sets recorded here.

Review Summary

Accuracy: all 335 served condition sets were turned into UniProtKB queries. In every set, every reviewed match carries the keyword Helicase; no reviewed match lacks it, and every set matches at least two reviewed entries. Meaning and parsimony: the sets select helicase families one by one (DEAD-box and DEAH-box RNA helicases, UvrD-like, RecQ-type and DnaB-like DNA helicases, MCM proteins, helicases of DNA and RNA viruses and others), and the keyword names exactly that enzyme class. Each signature is used in one set and no set is contained in another by its conditions.

Action Rationale

The rule is accurate for every set against Swiss-Prot and coherent as a statement about one enzyme class with a keyword at the right level. Its size reflects the number of helicase families, not padding. Overlap between sets by matched proteins was not measured, and unreviewed matches were counted, not inspected; confidence reflects both.

Biological Meaning

COHERENT

Not a GO rule, so projects/ARBA_REVIEW/rule_redundancy.tsv has no row. Judged by whether the sets select the enzyme class the keyword names: all 335 do, by the curated keyword of every reviewed match. KW-0347 has the parents Hydrolase and ATP-binding and no child keyword asserted by an ARBA rule, so no more specific keyword rule covers any set.

The consequent term has descendant terms. 0 condition set(s) are also matched by a rule asserting a more specific term. 335 condition set(s) were examined for this judgement.

Assessments

ACCEPTABLE

Each signature is used in exactly one set (309 FunFam, 157 InterPro and 35 PANTHER conditions over 335 sets; 220 sets are a single signature) and no set is contained in another by its conditions. Every set matches reviewed entries. Overlap by matched proteins was not measured, so some sets may add nothing beyond others, as sets 1 and 2 (both DEAD-box signatures, 1035 and 924 reviewed matches) suggest. No single InterPro entry covers all helicase superfamilies, so a multi-set rule is needed. keyword_redundancy.tsv: none of the 335 condition sets is matched by a rule for a descendant keyword.

APPROPRIATE

244 sets are unrestricted and 91 carry a taxon condition, including virus taxa for viral helicase FunFams (Papovaviricetes, Pisuviricota, Begomovirus) and Bacillati or Fungi for lineage-specific FunFams. Helicases occur in all cellular life and many viruses; no restriction was found that excludes a lineage where reviewed members of the set carry the keyword.

Corrections (5)

Claims made by an earlier version of this review that were withdrawn, with the evidence that refuted them.

Withdrawn claimCorrection
The rule lacks taxonomic restrictions, which is appropriate.
taxon misread · 2026-11-10		 91 of the 335 live condition sets carry a taxon condition; the previous text had recorded no condition sets.
  • Count over conditionSets of rest.uniprot.org/arba/ARBA00022806, 2026-11-09
  • Previous file recorded condition_sets: []
The rule should be modified by consolidating functionally redundant condition sets.
other · 2026-11-10		 The same text assessed condition overlap as none and stated the rule lacks redundancy; no redundant pair was named.
  • Previous condition_overlap assessment: NONE
355 condition sets are excessive, so the rule needs modification.
size as evidence · 2026-11-10		 Rule size is not evidence about correctness; the live rule has 335 sets.
  • rest.uniprot.org/arba/ARBA00022806, 2026-11-09: 335 sets
Each InterPro entry appears in only one or two condition sets, so the sets do not overlap.
unexamined rule · 2026-11-10		 How often an identifier is reused says nothing about how many proteins two sets share; no protein overlap was measured.
  • No pairwise protein counts were recorded
UNDECIDED: two of 335 condition sets were read and none was checked against annotated proteins.
meaning not judged · 2026-11-12		 All 335 sets were checked against Swiss-Prot and agree with curated keyword use, and the rule was judged as a statement: one enzyme class and the keyword that names it.
  • UniProtKB 2026_04 per-set counts in rule.condition_sets notes (2026-11-12)
  • rest.uniprot.org/keywords/KW-0347: parents KW-0378 Hydrolase and KW-0067 ATP-binding
